  <div class="devsite-article-body clearfix
  ">

  
    
    








<p>
</p>



<p>

    
      <div class="applicable-devices-box">
        <div class="applicable-devices-box-text">
          <div class="applicable-devices-box-title">
            Applicable XR devices
          </div>
          <div class="applicable-devices-box-description">
            This guidance helps you build experiences for these types of XR devices.
          </div>
          <div class="applicable-devices-box-link applicable-devices-box-flex-link">
            <a href="/develop/xr/devices">Learn about XR device types ΓåÆ</a>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="applicable-devices-icon-container flex-push-right"></div>
        
        
        
          <div class="applicable-devices-icon-container">
            <figure>
              <picture>
                <source
                  class="devsite-dark-theme"
                  srcset="https://developer.android.com/static/images/develop/xr/ai-glasses-icon_dt.svg"
                  media="(prefers-color-scheme: dark)"
                  alt="">
                <img class="applicable-devices-box-icon" alt src="/static/images/develop/xr/ai-glasses-icon.svg">
              </picture>
              <figcaption>Audio &<br>Display Glasses</figcaption>
              <a class="applicable-devices-box-icon-link" href="/develop/xr/devices#audio-display"></a>
            </figure>
          </div>
        
        <div class="applicable-devices-box-link applicable-devices-box-block-link">
          <a href="/develop/xr/devices">Learn about XR device types ΓåÆ</a>
        </div>
      </div>
      </p>



<p>After you&#39;ve <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions">requested and been granted the necessary permissions</a>, your app
can access the hardware on the audio glasses or display glasses. The key to
accessing the glasses&#39; hardware (instead of the phone&#39;s hardware), is to use a
<a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/glasses/support-different-types#projected-context">projected context</a>.</p>

<p>There are two primary ways to get a projected context, depending on where your
code is executing:</p>

<h2 id="projected-activity" data-text="Get a projected context if your code is running in an projected activity" tabindex="-1">Get a projected context if your code is running in an projected activity</h2>

<p><a name="glasses-activity"></a></p>

<p>If your app&#39;s code is running from within your <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/glasses/support-different-types#activity-lifecycle">projected activity</a>, its own
activity context is already a <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/glasses/support-different-types#projected-context">projected context</a>. In this scenario, calls
made within that activity can already access the glasses&#39; hardware.</p>

<h2 id="phone-activity-service" data-text="Get a projected context for code running in a phone app component" tabindex="-1">Get a projected context for code running in a phone app component</h2>

<p>If a part of your app outside of your projected activity (such as a phone
activity or a service) needs to access the glasses&#39; hardware, it must explicitly
obtain a projected context. To do this, use the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#createProjectedDeviceContext(android.content.Context)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">createProjectedDeviceContext</code></a> method:</p>

<p>

<div></div><devsite-code><pre id="androidxr_projected_context_get_projected-code-sample" data-scope="androidxr_projected_context_get_projected" data-code-snippet="true" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_get_projected" class="devsite-click-to-copy notranslate" dir="ltr" is-upgraded syntax="Kotlin"><span class="devsite-syntax-nd">@OptIn</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ExperimentalProjectedApi</span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">::</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">class</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-kd">private</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">fun</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nf">getGlassesContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">context</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">:</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Context</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">):</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Context? </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">return</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">try</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// From a phone Activity or Service, get a context for the AI glasses.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">createProjectedDeviceContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">context</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">catch</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">:</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">IllegalStateException</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Log</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">TAG</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-s">"Failed to create projected device context"</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kc">null</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><div class="devsite-github-link nocode no-select nocontent"><a target="_top" href="https://github.com/android/snippets/blob/0c4e653dad7e22be6bbcc58a47101bd5071ea65d/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#L68-L77" class="gc-analytics-event" data-category="github_link" data-label="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#androidxr_projected_context_get_projected" data-code-snippet="true" data-git-revision="main" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_get_projected"><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedHardware</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">kt</span></a></div></pre></devsite-code></p>

<h3 id="validity" data-text="Check for validity" tabindex="-1">Check for validity</h3>

<p>Wrap the <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#createProjectedDeviceContext(android.content.Context)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">createProjectedDeviceContext</code></a> call within the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#isProjectedDeviceConnected(android.content.Context,kotlin.coroutines.CoroutineContext)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ProjectedContext.isProjectedDeviceConnected</code></a>. While this method returns
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">true</code>, the projected context remains valid to the connected device, and your
phone app activity or service (such as a <code translate="no" dir="ltr">CameraManager</code>) can access the AI
glasses hardware.</p>

<h3 id="device-disconnect" data-text="Clean up on disconnect" tabindex="-1">Clean up on disconnect</h3>

<p>The projected context is tied to the lifecycle of the connected device, so it is
destroyed when the device disconnects. When the device disconnects,
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#isProjectedDeviceConnected(android.content.Context,kotlin.coroutines.CoroutineContext)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ProjectedContext.isProjectedDeviceConnected</code></a> returns <code translate="no" dir="ltr">false</code>. Your app
should listen for this change and clean up any system services (such as a
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">CameraManager</code>) or resources that your app created using that projected
context.</p>

<h3 id="device-reconnect" data-text="Re-initialize on reconnect" tabindex="-1">Re-initialize on reconnect</h3>

<p>When the glasses reconnect, your app can obtain another projected
context instance using <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#createProjectedDeviceContext(android.content.Context)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">createProjectedDeviceContext</code></a>, and then
re-initialize any system services or resources using the new projected context.</p>

<h2 id="record-audio" data-text="Record audio with the glasses' microphone" tabindex="-1">Record audio with the glasses' microphone</h2>

<p>You can record audio from the glasses using two distinct methods:</p>

<ul>
<li>Use a <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/glasses/support-different-types#projected-context">projected context</a>.</li>
<li>Use Bluetooth Hands-Free Profile (HFP).</li>
</ul>

<h3 id="choose-recording-method" data-text="Choose a recording methods" tabindex="-1">Choose a recording methods</h3>

<p>The method you choose depends on whether you need high-fidelity, XR-specific
audio processing, or standard Bluetooth audio input.</p>

<table>
  <tr>
    <th>Recording method</th>
    <th>Microphone access</th>
    <th>Common use case</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>Projected Context</p></td>
    <td><p>Multiple microphones</p></td>
    <td>
      <p>Recording using a projected context lets your app access multiple microphones from the glasses and its specialized hardware features, such as:
      </p>
      <ul>
        <li>XR-specific spatialization.</li>
        <li>Advanced denoising.</li>
        <li>Voice separation that distinguishes between wearer's and
        bystander's voices.</li>
        <li>Maintain recording access in multidevice environments even when the glasses are not the active Bluetooth device.</li>
      </ul>
    </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
    <td><p>Bluetooth HFP</p></td>
    <td><p>Single microphone</p></td>
    <td>
      <p>Relies on the Bluetooth Hands-Free Profile (HFP) for immediate, out-of-the-box compatibility. In this mode, the glasses connect to the phone using standard Headset and Advanced Audio Distribution Profile (A2DP) <a href="/develop/connectivity/bluetooth/profiles">profiles</a>, functioning like a typical Bluetooth peripheral.</p>
      <p>If your app is already designed for standard Bluetooth recording, you can use this method to record audio from the glasses without integrating any XR-specific capabilities.</p>
    </td>
  </tr>
</table>

<h3 id="record-audio-projected-context" data-text="Record audio using a projected context" tabindex="-1">Record audio using a projected context</h3>

<p>To record audio using a projected context, first request the required runtime
permissions, and then record the audio using the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord</code></a> API, as
described in the following sections.</p>

<h4 id="record-audio-projected-context-permissions" data-text="Request runtime permissions" tabindex="-1">Request runtime permissions</h4>

<p>To access multiple microphones on the glasses, you must request audio
permissions specifically for the projected device. The standard, phone-scoped
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">RECORD_AUDIO</code> permission that a user has granted for your app on their mobile
device is insufficient.</p>

<p>Follow these steps to request the permissions:</p>

<ol>
<li><a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions#declare-permissions">Declare the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">RECORD_AUDIO</code> permission</a> in your app&#39;s manifest file.</li>
<li><p>Request the projected-device-scoped permissions in one of the following
ways, depending on where your code is executing:</p>

<ul>
<li><strong>Code executing from a projected activity</strong>: Use the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/activity/result/ActivityResultLauncher"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ActivityResultLauncher</code></a> with the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/permissions/ProjectedPermissionsResultContract#ProjectedPermissionsResultContract()"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ProjectedPermissionsResultContract</code></a>. For more information on
using this method, see the <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions#register">register the permissions launcher</a>
section and subsequent sections in the guide for requesting hardware
permissions.</li>
<li><strong>Code executing from a host phone activity</strong>: Use
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/app/Activity#requestpermissions_1"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">Activity#requestPermissions(permissions, requestCode, deviceId)</code></a>
and provide the device ID obtained from your <code translate="no" dir="ltr">projectedDeviceContext</code>,
as described in the <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions#permissions-user-flow">understand the permission request user flow</a>
section of the guide for requesting hardware permissions.</li>
</ul></li>
</ol>

<h4 id="initialize-audiorecord" data-text="Initialize AudioRecord with a projected context" tabindex="-1">Initialize AudioRecord with a projected context</h4>

<p>To ensure that audio is recorded from the glasses rather than the host phone,
you must associate the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord</code> object with the projected device context.</p>

<p>The following code uses the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord.Builder"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord.Builder</code></a> and passes the
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">projectedDeviceContext</code> to the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord.Builder#setcontext"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">setContext</code></a> method:</p>

<p>

<div></div><devsite-code><pre id="androidxr_projected_context_audio_record-code-sample" data-scope="androidxr_projected_context_audio_record" data-code-snippet="true" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_audio_record" class="devsite-click-to-copy notranslate" dir="ltr" is-upgraded syntax="Kotlin"><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// Initialize AudioRecord with projected device context</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">audioRecord</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">AudioRecord</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">Builder</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setAudioSource</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">MediaRecorder</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">AudioSource</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">CAMCORDER</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setAudioFormat</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">audioFormat</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setBufferSizeInBytes</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">bufferSize</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// pass in the projected device context</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">projectedDeviceContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">build</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-n">audioRecord</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">startRecording</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span><div class="devsite-github-link nocode no-select nocontent"><a target="_top" href="https://github.com/android/snippets/blob/0c4e653dad7e22be6bbcc58a47101bd5071ea65d/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#L217-L226" class="gc-analytics-event" data-category="github_link" data-label="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#androidxr_projected_context_audio_record" data-code-snippet="true" data-git-revision="main" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_audio_record"><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedHardware</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">kt</span></a></div></pre></devsite-code></p>

<h5 id="key-points-initialize-audiorecord" data-text="Key points about the code" tabindex="-1">Key points about the code</h5>

<ul>
<li><p>You can set the audio source to <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/MediaRecorder.AudioSource#camcorder"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">CAMCORDER</code></a>,
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/MediaRecorder.AudioSource#voice_recognition"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">VOICE_RECOGNITION</code></a>, <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/MediaRecorder.AudioSource#voice_communication"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">VOICE_COMMUNICATION</code></a>, or
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/MediaRecorder.AudioSource#unprocessed"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">UNPROCESSED</code></a> to tailor the audio processing to your specific use
case.</p>

<p>For example, use <code translate="no" dir="ltr">VOICE_COMMUNICATION</code> if your use case needs automatic
noise reduction. <code translate="no" dir="ltr">VOICE_RECOGNITION</code> is processed with acoustic echo
cancellation (AEC). And if you need raw, unaltered audio, select
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">UNPROCESSED</code> or <code translate="no" dir="ltr">CAMCORDER</code>.</p></li>
<li><p>To ensure compatibility with the glasses, the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">audioFormat</code> object must
define a sample rate of 16kHz and a channel configuration of either mono or
stereo (using <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioFormat#channel_in_mono"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">CHANNEL_IN_MONO</code></a> or <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioFormat#channel_in_stereo"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">CHANNEL_IN_STEREO</code></a>).</p></li>
<li><p>Use <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord#getminbuffersize"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord.getMinBufferSize()</code></a> to determine the minimum buffer
size to create the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord</code> object. However, to prevent audio drops
from the glasses, you should read from this buffer in short, frequent chunks
(ideally 20ms slices) rather than waiting for the entire buffer to fill.</p></li>
</ul>
<aside class="preview"><strong>Preview:</strong><span> Support for using the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/MediaRecorder"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">MediaRecorder</code></a> API with a projected
context will be available in an upcoming Android Beta release.</span></aside>
<h5 id="initialize-audiorecord-cleanup" data-text="Clean up after use" tabindex="-1">Clean up after use</h5>

<p>When your app no longer needs the microphone, or when the activity is stopped,
call <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord#stop"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">stop</code></a> and <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord#release"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">release</code></a> on the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioRecord</code> object.</p>

<h5 id="initialize-audiorecord-check-permissions" data-text="Check for runtime permissions before recording" tabindex="-1">Check for runtime permissions before recording</h5>

<p>Before calling <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioRecord#startrecording"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">startRecording</code></a>, <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions#check-function">verify that the user has granted the
microphone permission for the glasses</a> using the projected context.</p>

<h3 id="record-audio-bluetooth" data-text="Record audio using Bluetooth HFP" tabindex="-1">Record audio using Bluetooth HFP</h3>

<p>To record audio using Bluetooth HFP, first request the required runtime
permissions, and then record the audio using the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioManager"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioManager</code></a> API, as
described in the following sections.</p>

<h4 id="record-audio-bluetooth-permissions" data-text="Request permissions" tabindex="-1">Request permissions</h4>

<p>As with any standard Bluetooth audio device, the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/Manifest.permission#record_audio"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">RECORD_AUDIO</code></a>,
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/Manifest.permission#bluetooth_connect"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">BLUETOOTH_CONNECT</code></a>, and other related permissions are controlled by the
phone and not the connected device (such as audio glasses or display glasses).</p>

<p>Follow these steps to request the permissions:</p>

<ol>
<li><p><a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/request-hardware-permissions#declare-permissions">Declare following permissions</a> in your app&#39;s manifest file:</p>

<ul>
<li><a href="/reference/kotlin/android/Manifest.permission#record_audio"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">RECORD_AUDIO</code></a></li>
<li><a href="/reference/kotlin/android/Manifest.permission#bluetooth_connect"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">BLUETOOTH_CONNECT</code></a></li>
<li><a href="/reference/kotlin/android/Manifest.permission#modify_audio_settings"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">MODIFY_AUDIO_SETTINGS</code></a></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p>Request both the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">RECORD_AUDIO</code> and <code translate="no" dir="ltr">BLUETOOTH_CONNECT</code> permissions at
runtime using the <a href="/training/permissions/requesting">standard Android permission flow</a>.</p></li>
</ol>

<h4 id="route-bluetooth-audio" data-text="Use AudioManager to route audio" tabindex="-1">Use AudioManager to route audio</h4>

<p>After the user has granted your app the necessary runtime permissions, use the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioManager"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">AudioManager</code></a> API to set the communication device to
<a href="/reference/kotlin/android/media/AudioDeviceInfo#type_bluetooth_sco"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">TYPE_BLUETOOTH_SCO</code></a> to route the audio through Bluetooth HFP. This
directs the system to retrieve audio from the Bluetooth peripheral.</p>

<p>

<h2 id="capture-image" data-text="Capture an image with the glasses' camera" tabindex="-1">Capture an image with the glasses' camera</h2>

<p>To capture an image with the glasses&#39; camera, set up and bind the CameraX&#39;s
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/core/ImageCapture"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code></a> <a href="/media/camera/camerax#ease-of-use">use case</a> to the glasses&#39; camera using the correct
context for your app:</p>

<p>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">cameraCharacteristics</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">camera2CameraInfo</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">getCameraCharacteristic</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// 4. Define the resolution strategy.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">targetResolution</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Size</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-m">1920</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-m">1080</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">resolutionStrategy</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ResolutionStrategy</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">targetResolution</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ResolutionStrategy</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">FALLBACK_RULE_CLOSEST_LOWER</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">resolutionSelector</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ResolutionSelector</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">Builder</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setResolutionStrategy</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">resolutionStrategy</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">build</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// 5. If you have other continuous use cases bound, such as Preview or ImageAnalysis,</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// you can use  Camera2 Interop's CaptureRequestOptions to set the FPS</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">fpsRange</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Range</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-m">30</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-m">60</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">captureRequestOptions</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">CaptureRequestOptions</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">Builder</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setCaptureRequestOption</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">CaptureRequest</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">CONTROL_AE_TARGET_FPS_RANGE</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">fpsRange</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">build</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// 6. Initialize the ImageCapture use case with options.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">val</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nv">imageCapture</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">=</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ImageCapture</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">Builder</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// Optional: Configure resolution, format, etc.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">setResolutionSelector</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">resolutionSelector</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">build</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">try</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// Unbind use cases before rebinding.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">cameraProvider</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">unbindAll</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">()</span>

<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// Bind use cases to camera using the Activity as the LifecycleOwner.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">cameraProvider</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">bindToLifecycle</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">                </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">activity</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">                </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">cameraSelector</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">                </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">imageCapture</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">catch</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">exc</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">:</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Exception</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">            </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Log</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">TAG</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-s">"Use case binding failed"</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">exc</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">},</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ContextCompat</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">getMainExecutor</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">activity</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">))</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><div class="devsite-github-link nocode no-select nocontent"><a target="_top" href="https://github.com/android/snippets/blob/0c4e653dad7e22be6bbcc58a47101bd5071ea65d/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#L103-L169" class="gc-analytics-event" data-category="github_link" data-label="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#androidxr_projected_camera_capture" data-code-snippet="true" data-git-revision="main" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_camera_capture"><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedHardware</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">kt</span></a></div></pre></devsite-code></p>

<h3 id="key-points-capture-image" data-text="Key points about the code" tabindex="-1">Key points about the code</h3>

<ul>
<li>Obtains an instance of the <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/lifecycle/ProcessCameraProvider"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ProcessCameraProvider</code></a> using the
<a href="#phone-activity-service"><strong>projected device context</strong></a>.</li>
<li>Within the projected context&#39;s scope, the glasses&#39; primary,
outward-pointing camera maps to the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">DEFAULT_BACK_CAMERA</code> when selecting a
camera.</li>
<li>A pre-binding check uses <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/lifecycle/ProcessCameraProvider#hasCamera(androidx.camera.core.CameraSelector)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">cameraProvider.hasCamera(cameraSelector)</code></a> to
verify that the selected camera is available on the device before
proceeding.</li>
<li>Uses <strong>Camera2 Interop</strong> with <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/camera2/interop/Camera2CameraInfo"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">Camera2CameraInfo</code></a> to read the
underlying <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/hardware/camera2/CameraCharacteristics#scaler_stream_configuration_map"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">CameraCharacteristics#SCALER_STREAM_CONFIGURATION_MAP</code></a>,
which can be useful for advanced checks on supported resolutions.</li>
<li>A custom <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/core/resolutionselector/ResolutionSelector"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ResolutionSelector</code></a> is built to precisely control the output
image resolution for <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/core/ImageCapture"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code></a>.</li>
<li>Creates an <code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code> use case that is configured with a custom
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">ResolutionSelector</code>.</li>
<li>Binds the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code> use case to the activity&#39;s lifecycle. This
automatically manages the opening and closing of the camera based on the
activity&#39;s state (for example, stopping the camera when the activity is
paused).</li>
</ul>

<p>After the glasses&#39; camera is set up, you can capture an image with the
CameraX&#39;s <code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code> class. Refer to the CameraX&#39;s documentation to learn
about using <a href="/media/camera/camerax/take-photo#take_a_picture"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">takePicture</code></a> to <a href="/media/camera/camerax/take-photo#take_a_picture">capture an image</a>.</p>

<h3 id="capture-video" data-text="Capture a video with the glasses' camera" tabindex="-1">Capture a video with the glasses' camera</h3>

<p>To capture a video instead of an image with the glasses&#39; camera, replace the
<code translate="no" dir="ltr">ImageCapture</code> components with the corresponding <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/camera/video/VideoCapture"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">VideoCapture</code></a> components
and modify the capture execution logic.</p>

<p>The main changes involve using a different use case, creating a different output
file, and initiating the capture using the appropriate video recording method.
For more information about the <code translate="no" dir="ltr">VideoCapture</code> API and how to use it, see the
<a href="/media/camera/camerax/video-capture#using-videocapture-api">CameraX&#39;s video capture documentation</a>.</p>
<aside class="special"><strong>Important:</strong><span> Battery power and thermal dissipation on glasses devices is often
limited. When developing for glasses, pay close attention to the requested
<strong>output format</strong> and <strong>resolution</strong>, as these can severely impact system power
and temperature.</span></aside>
<p>The following table shows the recommended resolution and frame rate depending on
your app&#39;s use case:</p>

<table>
    <tr>
        <th>Use case</th>
        <th>Resolution</th>
        <th>Frame rate</th>
    </tr>
    <tr>
        <td>Video Communication</td>
        <td>1280 x 720</td>
        <td>15 FPS</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
        <td>Computer Vision</td>
        <td>640 x 480</td>
        <td>10 FPS</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
        <td>AI Video Streaming</td>
        <td>640 x 480</td>
        <td>1 FPS</td>
    </tr>
</table>

<h2 id="access-phone-hardware" data-text="Access a phone's hardware from a projected activity" tabindex="-1">Access a phone's hardware from a projected activity</h2>

<p>An <a href="/develop/xr/jetpack-xr-sdk/glasses/support-different-types#activity-lifecycle">projected activity</a> can also access the phone&#39;s hardware (such as the
camera or microphone) by using <a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#createHostDeviceContext(android.content.Context)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">createHostDeviceContext(context)</code></a> to get
the host device&#39;s (phone) context:</p>

<p>

<div></div><devsite-code><pre id="androidxr_projected_context_get_host-code-sample" data-scope="androidxr_projected_context_get_host" data-code-snippet="true" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_get_host" class="devsite-click-to-copy notranslate" dir="ltr" is-upgraded syntax="Kotlin"><span class="devsite-syntax-nd">@OptIn</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ExperimentalProjectedApi</span><span class="devsite-syntax-o">::</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">class</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-kd">private</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kd">fun</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-nf">getPhoneContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">activity</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">:</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ComponentActivity</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">):</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Context? </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">return</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">try</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-c1">// From an AI glasses Activity, get a context for the phone.</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">createHostDeviceContext</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">activity</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-k">catch</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">:</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">IllegalStateException</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">{</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">Log</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">(</span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">TAG</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-s">"Failed to create host device context"</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">,</span><span class="devsite-syntax-w"> </span><span class="devsite-syntax-n">e</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">)</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">        </span><span class="devsite-syntax-kc">null</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-w">    </span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span>
<span class="devsite-syntax-p">}</span><div class="devsite-github-link nocode no-select nocontent"><a target="_top" href="https://github.com/android/snippets/blob/0c4e653dad7e22be6bbcc58a47101bd5071ea65d/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#L85-L94" class="gc-analytics-event" data-category="github_link" data-label="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt#androidxr_projected_context_get_host" data-code-snippet="true" data-git-revision="main" data-github-path="android/snippets/xr/src/main/java/com/example/xr/projected/ProjectedHardware.kt" data-region-tag="androidxr_projected_context_get_host"><span class="devsite-syntax-n">ProjectedHardware</span><span class="devsite-syntax-p">.</span><span class="devsite-syntax-na">kt</span></a></div></pre></devsite-code></p>

<p>When accessing hardware or resources that are specific to the host device
(phone) in a hybrid app (an app containing both mobile and glasses
experiences), you must explicitly select the correct context to make sure your
app can access the correct hardware:</p>

<ul>
<li>Use the <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/app/Activity"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">Activity</code></a> context from the phone <code translate="no" dir="ltr">Activity</code> or the
<a href="/reference/kotlin/androidx/xr/projected/ProjectedContext#createHostDeviceContext(android.content.Context)"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">ProjectedContext.createHostDeviceContext</code></a> to get the phone&#39;s
context.</li>
<li>Don&#39;t use <a href="/reference/kotlin/android/content/Context#getapplicationcontext"><code translate="no" dir="ltr">getApplicationContext</code></a> because the application context
can incorrectly return the glasses&#39; context if a projected activity was the
most-recently-launched component.</li>
</ul>
